14f:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj branch"}]," has been deprecated in favor of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj bookmark"}],"."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Rationale:"}]," Jujutsu's branches don't behave like Git branches, which a\nconfused many newcomers, as they expected a similar behavior given the name.\nWe've renamed them to \"bookmarks\" to match the actual behavior, as we think\nthat describes them better, and they also behave similar to Mercurial's\nbookmarks."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj obslog"}]," is now called ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj evolution-log"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj evolog"}],". ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj obslog"}]," remains\nas an alias."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj unsquash"}]," has been deprecated in favor of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj squash"}]," and\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj diffedit --restore-descendants"}],"."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Rationale:"}]," ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj squash"}]," can be used in interactive mode to pull\nchanges from one commit to another, including from a parent commit\nto a child commit. For fine-grained dependent diffs, such as when\nthe parent and the child commits must successively modify the same\nlocation in a file, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj diffedit --restore-descendants"}]," can be used\nto set the parent commit to the desired content without altering the\ncontent of the child commit."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git.push-branch-prefix"}]," config has been deprecated in favor of\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"git.push-bookmark-prefix"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"conflict()"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"file()"}]," revsets have been renamed to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"conflicts()"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"files()"}],"\nrespectively. The old names are still around and will be removed in a future\nrelease."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]

185:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Previously, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git push"}]," only made sure that the branch is in the expected\nlocation on the remote server when pushing a branch forward (as opposed to\nsideways or backwards). Now, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git push"}]," makes a safety check in all cases\nand fails whenever ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git fetch"}]," would have introduced a conflict."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["In other words, previously branches that moved sideways or backward were\npushed similarly to Git's ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git push --force"}],"; now they have protections\nsimilar to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git push --force-with-lease"}]," (though not identical to it, to match\nthe behavior of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git fetch"}],"). Note also that because of the way ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git fetch"}]," works, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj"}]," does not suffer from the same problems as Git's ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git push --force-with-lease"}]," in situations when ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git fetch"}]," is run in the background."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":"When the working copy commit becomes immutable, a new one is automatically\ncreated\non top of it to avoid letting the user edit the immutable one."}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj config list"}]," now properly escapes TOML keys (#1322)."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":"Files with conflicts are now checked out as executable if all sides of the\nconflict are executable."}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The progress bar (visible when using e.g. ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git clone"}],") clears the\nremainder of the cursor row after drawing rather than clearing the entire row\nbefore drawing, eliminating the \"flicker\" effect seen on some terminals."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]

1b0:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj checkout"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj merge"}]," are both deprecated; use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}]," instead to\nreplace both of these commands in all instances."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Rationale"}],": ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj checkout"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj merge"}]," both implement identical\nfunctionality, which is a subset of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}],". ",["$","code",null,{"children":"checkout"}]," creates a new working\ncopy commit on top of a single specified revision, i.e. with one parent.\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"merge"}]," creates a new working copy commit on top of ",["$","em",null,{"children":"at least"}]," two specified\nrevisions, i.e. with two or more parents."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The only difference between these commands and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}],", which ",["$","em",null,{"children":"also"}]," creates\na new working copy commit, is that ",["$","code",null,{"children":"new"}]," can create a working copy commit on\ntop of any arbitrary number of revisions, so it can handle both the previous\ncases at once. The only actual difference between these three commands is the\ncommand syntax and their name. These names were chosen to be familiar to users\nof other version control systems, but we instead encourage all users to adopt\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}]," instead; it is more general and easier to remember than both of\nthese."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj checkout"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj merge"}]," will no longer be shown as part of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj help"}],", but\nwill still function for now, emitting a warning about their deprecation."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Deadline"}],": ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj checkout"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj merge"}]," will be deleted and are expected\nbecome a ",["$","strong",null,{"children":"hard error later in 2024"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj init --git"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj init --git-repo"}]," are now deprecated and will be\nremoved\nin the near future."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git init"}]," instead."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]

1e3:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":"The minimum supported Rust version (MSRV) is now 1.71.0."}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":"The storage format of branches, tags, and git refs has changed. Newly-stored\nrepository data will no longer be loadable by older binaries."}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":":"}]," revset operator is deprecated. Use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"::"}]," instead. We plan to delete the\n",["$","code",null,{"children":":"}]," form in jj 0.15+."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--allow-large-revsets"}]," flag for ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj rebase"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}]," was replaced by\na ",["$","code",null,{"children":"all:"}]," before the revset. For example, use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj rebase -d 'all:foo-'"}],"\ninstead of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj rebase --allow-large-revsets -d 'foo-'"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--allow-large-revsets"}]," flag for ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj rebase"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}]," can no longer be\nused for allowing duplicate destinations. Include the potential duplicates\nin a single expression instead (e.g. ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new 'all:x|y'"}],")."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"push.branch-prefix"}]," option was renamed to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git.push-branch-prefix"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The default editor on Windows is now ",["$","code",null,{"children":"Notepad"}]," instead of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"pico"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj"}]," will fail attempts to snapshot new files larger than 1MiB by default.\nThis behavior\ncan be customized with the ",["$","code",null,{"children":"snapshot.max-new-file-size"}]," config option."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Author and committer signatures now use empty strings to represent unset\nnames and email addresses. The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"author"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"committer"}]," template keywords and\nmethods also return empty strings.\nOlder binaries may not warn user when attempting to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git push"}]," commits\nwith such signatures."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["In revsets, the working-copy or remote symbols (such as ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"workspace_id@"}],",\nand ",["$","code",null,{"children":"branch@remote"}],") can no longer be quoted as a unit. If a workspace or\nbranch name contains whitespace, quote the name like ",["$","code",null,{"children":"\"branch name\"@remote"}],".\nAlso, these symbols will not be resolved as revset aliases or function\nparameters. For example, ",["$","code",null,{"children":"author(foo@)"}]," is now an error, and the revset alias\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"'revset-aliases.foo@' = '@'"}]," will be failed to parse."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"root"}]," revset symbol has been converted to function ",["$","code",null,{"children":"root()"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"..x"}]," revset is now evaluated to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"root()..x"}],", which means the root commit\nis no longer included."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git push"}]," will now push all branches in the range ",["$","code",null,{"children":"remote_branches()..@"}],"\ninstead of only branches pointing to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@"}]," or ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@-"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["It's no longer allowed to create a Git remote named \"git\". Use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git remote rename"}]," to rename the existing remote.\n","$L2a7"]}],"\n"]}],"\n","$L2a8","\n"]}]

This can be\nused with ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj split"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj move -i"}],", etc."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj log"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"obslog"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"op log"}]," now supports ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--limit N"}]," option to show the first\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"N"}]," entries."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Added the ",["$","code",null,{"children":"ui.paginate"}]," option to enable/disable pager usage in commands"]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj checkout"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj describe"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj commit"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj new"}],"/",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj squash"}]," can take repeated\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"-m/--message"}]," arguments. The goal is to encourage users to use the change ID instead, since\nthat is generally more convenient, and it reduces the risk of creating\ndivergent commits."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The username and hostname that appear in the operation log are now\nconfigurable via config options ",["$","code",null,{"children":"operation.username"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"operation.hostname"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git"}]," subcommands now support credential helpers."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj log"}]," will warn if it appears that the provided path was meant to be a\nrevset."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The new global flag ",["$","code",null,{"children":"-v/--verbose"}]," will turn on debug logging to give\nsome additional insight into what is happening behind the scenes.\nNote: This is not comprehensively supported by all operations yet."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj log"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj show"}],", and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj obslog"}]," now all support showing relative\ntimestamps by setting ",["$","code",null,{"children":"ui.relative-timestamps = true"}]," in the config file."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]

Earlier, files present in the\nsubmodule directory in the working copy would become added (tracked), and\nlater removed if you checked out another commit. You can now use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"git"}]," to\npopulate the submodule directory and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj"}]," will leave it alone."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Git's GC could remove commits that were referenced from jj in some cases. The output\nnow lists all parents and the diff summary is against the auto-merged parents."]}],"\n"]}],"\n"]}]

211:["$","ul",null,{"children":["\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The new ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj interdiff"}]," command compares the changes in commits, ignoring\nchanges from intervening commits."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj rebase"}]," now accepts a ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--branch/-b <revision>"}]," argument, which can be used\ninstead of ",["$","code",null,{"children":"-r"}]," or ",["$","code",null,{"children":"-s"}]," to specify which commits to rebase. It will rebase the\nwhole branch, relative to the destination. The default mode has changed from\n",["$","code",null,{"children":"-r @"}]," to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"-b @"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The new ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj print"}]," command prints the contents of a file in a revision."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The new ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj git remotes list"}]," command lists the configured remotes and their\nURLs.\n","$L2ec"]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj move"}]," and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj squash"}]," now lets you limit the set of changes to move by\nspecifying paths on the command line (in addition to the ",["$","code",null,{"children":"--interactive"}],"\nmode). For example, use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj move --to @-- foo"}]," to move the changes to file\n(or directory) ",["$","code",null,{"children":"foo"}]," in the working copy to the grandparent commit."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["When ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj move/squash/unsquash"}]," abandons the source commit because it became\nempty and both the source and the destination commits have non-empty\ndescriptions, it now asks for a combined description. If either description\nwas empty, it uses the other without asking."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","code",null,{"children":"jj split"}]," now lets you specify on the CLI which paths to include in the first\ncommit. The interactive diff-editing is not started when you do that."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Sparse checkouts are now supported. In fact, all working copies are now\n\"sparse\", only to different degrees. Use the ",["$","code",null,{"children":"jj sparse"}]," command to manage\nthe paths included in the sparse checkout."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Configuration is now also read from ",["$","code",null,{"children":"~/.jjconfig.toml"}],"."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["The ",["$","code",null,{"children":"$$JJ_CONFIG"}]," environment variable can now point to a directory.
